  pushed SHA before the operator is interrupted.
- The subsequent narrow operator FOV passes before full SV-0 begins. No
  aggregate or candidate construction has run.

##### M9.b.5.2 — Grounded advisory synthesis and join coverage

`[code]` `[docs]` `[adr]` `[gate]` `[audit]` `[external]`

- Amend ADR 0021/0083 before or with executable work: layout-only composition
  cannot satisfy a request for substantive cross-child synthesis. Preserve the
  recoverable central composer and one stored report, but let a temporary
  private Jido synthesis lifecycle produce one bounded advisory synthesis from
  the original request, accepted child observations, and parent-only join
  guidance. The model may explain relationships and answer the joined request;
  it still cannot alter or author child status, failure/cancellation truth,
  effect receipts, authoritative observations, ordering, identifiers, delivery,
  actions, permissions, or new work.
- M9.b.5's layout-v1/content-free prose contract remains authoritative for
  validation and replay of already-selected v1 reports only. This milestone
  supersedes it for every new selection; no new report may write layout v1.
  `AllbertAssist.Objectives.Fanout.ReportComposer` remains the plain-GenServer
  recoverable queue, claim, CAS, and retry owner. A private ephemeral
  `ReportComposer.SynthesisAgent` and `ReportComposer.Commands.Synthesize` own
  the one Jido transition inside a claimed composition;
  `ReportComposer.ReqLLMImplementation` owns the single structured provider
  call; and `Fanout.Report` alone validates versions, allocates bytes, renders
  deterministic sections, and verifies stored selection/body provenance. No
  second composer service, action, queue, table, or setting is introduced. One
  small pure `Fanout.Report.SynthesisPolicy` module owns the immutable contract
  version plus ordered rule specifications/ids; the prompt/schema, local
  validator, and model/fallback provenance all consume that one catalog rather
  than copying policy across three modules. Both this catalog and Worker
  QualityPolicy use the shared pure
  `AllbertAssist.Models.ClosedRuleEvidence` transport/normalization boundary;
  each policy still owns the meaning and applicability of its own rules.
- Extend the typed composer contract to layout v2 while preserving validated v1
  reads/replay for already-durable selections; new selections write only v2.
  Synthesis-contract version 1 is the immutable local rule catalog for complete
  child coverage, parent join-request coverage, relationship support, internal
  consistency, uncertainty, no effect/authority claims, and one-paragraph
  presentation.
  Snapshot/input-digest v2 adds exact fields `result_authority` and nullable
  `quality_receipt_sha256` to every child envelope. Authority is the closed
  `reviewed_advisory | registered_action | legacy_unreviewed_advisory`:
  reviewed DirectAnswer requires the receipt digest; a non-DirectAnswer action
  requires no quality receipt and keeps its existing exact effect-receipt field;
  its persisted action name must still resolve through the central Action
  Registry before v2 may call it `registered_action`. Unknown/corrupt action
  identity fails the v2 freeze rather than manufacturing report authority, while
  an already-selected v1 report is verified/replayed through its frozen v1
  contract before this new check so plugin removal cannot rewrite history. A
  completed observation requires exact `child.current_step_id == step.id`,
  `step.objective_id == child.id`, and completed Step status. Non-completed
  children are different: cancellation, stale abandonment, and retry-exhaustion
  recovery can leave a valid proposed/selected/running/blocked or terminal
  current Step. Verify the exact ownership and real schema-valid Step status,
  reject any completion/quality-receipt authority, Registry-resolve a persisted
  non-DirectAnswer action before naming it, and keep it outside synthesis. A
  missing Step is accepted only when `current_step_id` is nil. A completed
  registered-action child must consume the existing exact Lifecycle completion
  event with `summary` equal to the existing 500-character Lifecycle projection
  of `child.last_observation_summary`, with that durable Objective summary byte-
  equal to the completed Step's `result_summary`, matching `step_id`, and
  `step_status=completed`, and no quality-receipt key; do not invent a
  summary-only report contract or let mismatched prose inherit action authority.
  Every Lifecycle terminal path validates the transient Step belongs to that
  child before persisting `current_step_id` or finalizing the Step, including
  non-final-sibling writes; final report freeze remains a second check rather
  than the first ownership boundary.
  A non-completed row renders terminal detail, states that no completed result
  exists, and never forces fallback merely because its DirectAnswer quality
  receipt is inapplicable. A pre-contract DirectAnswer completion is retained
  as legacy-unreviewed and
  forces a v2 deterministic fallback with reason `legacy_unreviewed_children`
  rather than a model call or stranded parent. The same fail-safe classification
  applies to known pre-fix v1.3 events when `current_step_id` is nil: accept only
  exact `{summary}`, `{summary, step_id}`,
  `{summary, step_id, step_status=completed}`, or
  `{summary, step_id, step_status=completed, quality_receipt}` payloads after
  validating the referenced Step's child ownership, completed status, durable
  result summary, and any receipt binding when a Step is named. The summary-only
  shape must exactly match the existing 500-character projection of the durable
  Objective summary and, because it binds no Step, action, or receipt, establishes
  none of those authorities. Never reconstruct reviewed or registered authority,
  and reject empty/extra/unknown payloads. If no child
  completed, synthesis has no accepted observation substrate and makes zero
  provider calls; v2 stores the complete deterministic status/attention report
  with fallback reason `no_completed_children` and outcome `not_run`. Its
  structured provider result has exactly `sections`,
  `advisory_synthesis`, and `review`; `review` has exactly `rule_violations`
  and `covered_queue_positions`. The catalog-derived raw JSON Schema requires
  one Boolean property for every synthesis-rule id and rejects additional
  properties. The prompt renders those same stable ids beside their policy-owned
  instructions, and every property description defines `true` as a violation
  remaining in the returned final output after revision and `false` as
  satisfied or not applicable. Local code stamps the version, reconstructs
  ordered normalized rule results, derives failed ids and the aggregate verdict,
  and accepts only
  an empty failed-id set plus every completed queue position once in ascending
  durable order. The provider never authors the aggregate verdict, rule ids, or
  rule order. Sections preserve
  provider order; each section preserves its ordered positions. Every synthesis
  thus binds the exact completed-child queue positions and a closed review of the
  original request/join coverage. Allbert deterministically owns the report
  heading, status totals, attention section, explicit
  `model-authored advisory synthesis` label, authoritative child appendix,
  receipt language, byte allocation, input/selection/body digests, durable
  selection, and surface projection. Advisory synthesis is never effect
  evidence and cannot replace, summarize away, or mutate a child observation.
  Before whitespace/control normalization, compare the exact raw model
  synthesis with Redactor output. Detected secret material fails to
  deterministic fallback; never silently mutate the prose and preserve a review
  verdict or digest that covered different bytes.
  The v2 appendix makes that receipt language operator-visible for every child:
  `reviewed_advisory` names the verified quality-receipt digest and says review
  is advisory quality rather than effect evidence; a completed
  `registered_action` says semantic review is not applicable and leaves effect
  truth on the existing effect-receipt line; a non-completed row displays only
  terminal detail and the absence of a completed result; and a completed
  `legacy_unreviewed_advisory` says the quality receipt is absent and
  deterministic fallback is required. Its observation label matches status and
  authority without promoting terminal detail to a result. The operator-derived
  parent title plus every
  model-authored child title, objective, and observation/detail are untrusted
  display data: layout v2 renders each occurrence through one deterministic,
  reversible JSON-string encoding before any Allbert-authored status,
  authority, quality-receipt, or effect-receipt line. Embedded newlines or
  case variants of those labels therefore remain visibly quoted data and cannot
  forge a receipt boundary. This is structural encoding, not a domain regex or
  semantic oracle. Layout-v1 rendering stays byte-exact.
- Exact v2 model provenance keys are `model_profile`, `provider`, `model`,
  `layout_version=2`, `sections`, `synthesis_contract_version`,
  `review_verdict=accepted`, `reviewed_queue_positions`, and
  `synthesis_sha256`. The v2 selection-
  digest domain binds that provenance; the v2 input-digest domain binds the
  receipt-bearing snapshot. The synthesis itself lives only in the one stored
  report body between deterministic label/appendix delimiters; verification
  extracts that exact paragraph, checks its digest and closed syntax, and
  re-renders the complete body. New deterministic fallback provenance uses
  exactly `fallback_reason`, `layout_version=2`,
  `synthesis_contract_version`, and `synthesis_outcome=not_run | unresolved`;
  `legacy_unreviewed_children` and `no_completed_children` are the additive
  no-call reasons for the pre-contract and zero-success cases above. It contains
  no model prose. `recovery_after_restart` always records `unresolved`, because
  a stranded `composing` row cannot prove whether its one provider call began;
  it never claims `not_run` from absence of a durable selection. Existing v1
  digest domains/provenance remain read-only compatibility paths.
- A v1 digest on an unselected `queued` or stranded `composing` parent is not a
  new-v1-write exception. In the existing immediate transaction, first verify
  the old v1 snapshot/digest, derive and validate the authority-bearing v2
  snapshot, then compare-and-swap the same `report_input_digest` from v1 to v2
  only while delivery is `not_ready` and selection/body/source/delivery-receipt
  fields remain empty. A queued claim combines that rebind with
  `queued -> composing`; recovery rebinds a stranded composition before writing
  the exact v2 `recovery_after_restart` fallback. A crash after rebind leaves a
  recoverable v2 `composing` row. Already-selected/pending/delivered v1 state is
  never rebound. Because the join event is append-only, projection after the CAS
  verifies its immutable `fanout_joined` digest against exact frozen v1 bytes

  event mismatch still fails inconsistent. Historical pending/delivered backfill
  remains on its explicit byte-exact v1 compatibility path. No migration column,
  table, event kind, or second snapshot is added.
- Both v2 digests use `Objectives.CanonicalJSON`. Their exact ASCII domain tags
  are `allbert:fanout-report-input:v2\0` and
  `allbert:fanout-report-selection:v2\0`; the latter hashes the canonical map
  with exact keys `source` and `provenance`. `synthesis_sha256` is the ordinary
  SHA-256 of the normalized UTF-8 paragraph so the operator can independently
  compare it with the stored body. Layout-v1 keeps its existing v1 tags and
  canonical encoding byte-for-byte.
- The deterministic v2 body delimiters are the line
  `Model-authored advisory synthesis:` and the existing two-line break before
  `Effect verification comes only from the authoritative child-results appendix below.`
  The normalized synthesis is the one non-empty paragraph between them. Those
  literals make extraction/digest verification unambiguous without parsing or
  trusting model-authored headings.
- Replace the fixed 1,024-byte request prefix and 128-byte per-child composition
  excerpts with a separate fair model-input allocator inside the existing 16
  KiB envelope. Give the complete already-bounded original request priority so
  trailing join guidance is visible, then carry complete accepted child
  observations whenever they fit and explicitly shorten every affected child
  only when the input bound requires it. The 16 KiB limit is measured on the
  final canonical JSON bytes after escaping. Fixed input priority is the
  complete 4,000-character parent request (including multibyte text and trailing
  join guidance; this source-domain bound is characters, not bytes),
  then every child's queue position/identity plus explicit marker-floor task
  fields, then the common accepted-observation floor and largest fair evidence
  expansion, and only then optional objective/expected-result expansion from
  remaining bytes. The provider is not called if even that closed minimum
  evidence envelope cannot fit. The composer must not be asked to
  satisfy guidance or synthesize nuance it was never shown. This advisory-input
  allocator is separate from the 32 KiB durable report allocator and never
  changes canonical child truth.
- Use a bounded `deterministic_baseline -> review_and_revise -> accepted |
  unresolved` synthesis lifecycle under the existing single composer call and
  plan deadline. The one structured provider result carries the final advisory
  synthesis plus closed rule evidence; local code rejects an omitted child,
  missing join obligation, contradiction reported by the review, malformed
  schema, or any locally normalized violated rule. An unresolved review selects
  a truthful deterministic fallback with an explicit reason and all
  authoritative child truth; it never reports healthy semantic synthesis. No
  uncounted critique or repair call is added. Typed selection provenance binds
  the review contract, verdict, child positions, synthesis digest, and layout
  version.
  `ReportComposer` owns one killable Task around the entire private Jido
  lifecycle and caps it with the already-authorized effective composer timeout.
  Jido `cmd timeout: 0` is permitted only inside that owned Task; expiry
  terminates it before selecting `synthesis_timeout/unresolved`, because the one
  call may have crossed the provider boundary. Pre-dispatch exhaustion remains
  `deadline_exhausted/not_run`. An unexpected Task exit stays crash-visible and
  restart recovery records `recovery_after_restart/unresolved`; no provider or
  Jido child may outlive the selection attempt.
- Give authoritative evidence first claim on the report byte budget. Compute
  the synthesis allowance only after deterministic headings, status/attention,
  receipts, and the complete under-bound appendix fit. Reject the advisory
  synthesis or select fallback before shortening a child merely to make room
  for model prose. Preserve M9.b.5.1's fair maximum-child shortening policy;
  normalize synthesis to one paragraph of at most 4,096 UTF-8 bytes and require
  that exact reviewed paragraph to fit unchanged in the computed allowance. If
  it does not, select `fanout_report_synthesis_too_large/unresolved`; never
  shorten accepted prose or stamp accepted provenance over different bytes.
  For the separate 16 KiB model input, keep the complete bounded parent request
  fixed. Hold optional child objective/expected-result fields at their explicit
  marker floor while allocating the largest common cap across every completed
  observation. Preserve observations of at most 64 UTF-8 bytes completely and
  at least a readable 64-byte content prefix plus marker for every longer
  observation. Only then expand the optional task fields from remaining bytes
  under a second common cap. If all evidence prefixes cannot fit, make no
  provider call and select `composition_input_too_large/not_run`.
  Compute the deterministic-fallback child allocation before model rendering.
  Relationship sections and synthesis may not worsen any child's title,
  objective, or detail shortening vector versus that baseline; if they would
  displace otherwise fitting authoritative truth, select fallback. Render the
  normalized synthesis inside one fixed Markdown blockquote beneath its
  Allbert-owned advisory label. Structural framing, not a substring/regex
  denylist, is the visual authority boundary; the closed synthesis rules own
  semantic no-spoof quality. Bind the digest to the exact inner paragraph and
  validate the exact frame during extraction. The v2
  provenance plus `fanout_report_selected` payload must still fit the existing
  2,000-character event-payload changeset ceiling at the 16-child maximum, with
  a separate encoded-byte assertion recorded by the boundary test.
- Add independent rows for missing complement repaired, omitted child rejected,
  attempted status/receipt rewriting ignored, contradictory synthesis
  unresolved, malformed/timeout/provider failure, deterministic fallback,
  zero-completed no-call, v1 queued/composing CAS upgrade, restart/replay/tamper,
  a real Lifecycle/terminal-transition registered-action completion,
  unknown-action rejection, maximum-body allocation, and byte-identical TUI/Web/
  DM/public-protocol projection. Model and fallback rows each include multiline
  parent/child titles and child objectives/observations containing forged
  status/authority/quality/effect labels and prove those bytes remain
  JSON-string data rather than becoming deterministic receipt lines.
  Receipt/action/input integrity failures are
  classified through one explicit allowlist shared by queue scan and recovery:
  each corrupt parent is represented in a bounded aggregate and left untouched
  while later valid parents
  continue, whereas an unclassified Repo/operational failure still aborts.
  Both paths cursor through deterministic 100-row batches and emit at most one
  content-free aggregate per typed reason per batch, retaining its count,
  first/last id, and complete ordered `parent_ids` list. The batch cap bounds the
  record, while an id range or count alone remains
  inadequate operator evidence. Validate parent shape, terminal child shape, and the absolute
  16-child limit before querying Steps or completion events, so an oversized or
  corrupt parent is a classified skip rather than a SQLite bind-limit or head-
  of-line failure. Both
  model and fallback rows assert the exact v2 authority/quality-receipt language
  and keep it byte-identical on every projection. The
  configured real-provider matrix spans
  complementary, contrasting, sequential, supporting, independent, partial,
  and unrelated-domain cases; human semantic judgment remains explicit.
- Dev-gate RED/green tests first prove that the old layout-v1 evaluator rejects
  a valid layout-v2 result, then remove that false path. The replacement fixture
  carries valid reviewed-advisory receipt digests and exercises the production
  `SynthesisAgent` plus `prepare_synthesis/2`; its fake and configured-provider
  rows assert closed stage/reason diagnostics, exact v2 body/provenance/
  selection digests, every required violation property, local verdict
  derivation, and deterministic fallback for any applicable violation. Layout-
  v1 replay remains read-only and byte-exact.
- Rejoin every central report consumer through one shared public-path fixture
  that creates schema-valid Step/event authority and exact layout-v2 selection
  provenance. TUI, TUI subscriptions, Notify/DM, Web action/LiveView, and public
  protocol streaming/non-streaming rows each cover `model` and
  `deterministic_fallback`, reuse one multiline forged-label corpus, and compare
  the emitted report byte-for-byte with the reloaded parent `report_body`.
  Production validation is never relaxed to accommodate a fixture. Carry a
  maximum-bound 32,768-byte stored report through the real TUI session codec,
  cumulative acknowledgement, and renderer using ADR 0091's existing typed
  authority-output seam; ordinary output/input remain governed by
  `channels.tui.max_text_bytes`, and the unchanged 48 KiB `lines`/64 KiB frame
  bounds remain hard limits. An over-bound authority rejection proves the wire
  closes with content-free `:overflow`, the caller receives only the typed
  reason, and no report marker appears in an OTP crash/last-message log.

Acceptance:

- The exact FOV-4 report explains the substantive complement in one bounded
  advisory synthesis, retains both accepted child observations exactly once in
  the deterministic authoritative appendix, and preserves all status/receipt
  truth. Missing join coverage or an unresolved review is visible degradation,
  not success.
- ADRs, plan/request flow, focused owner/recovery/surface tests, targeted static
  checks, configured-provider matrix, fresh clean-SHA agent FOV, and independent
  audit agree before the operator is asked to repeat FOV. No aggregate, package,
  full SV, or unrelated validation runs in this milestone.

#### M9.b — Docs, cumulative gate, FV, binary release, and closeout

`[docs]` `[gate]` `[external]` `[fv]`

- Enter only after M9.b.1 through M9.b.5 are complete, the independent
  remediation audits are clear, focused fan-out qualification passed, and the
  subsequent full source validation is accepted. Check every
  Purpose outcome against landed behavior; update request flow, ADR statuses,
  operator/developer docs, CHANGELOG, version lockstep, roadmap/backlog lifecycle,
  and release evidence.
- Add `release.v13 = release.v121 + v1.3 focused contracts` under
  `release_evidence/v13/`.
- At one pushed clean provisional candidate SHA structurally verify that
  `release.v13` contains `release.v121` and every older versioned gate as its
  exact ordered prefix, but do not execute either aggregate yet. Use M9.a2 to
  construct and qualify one complete local three-target draft generation at
  that exact SHA, stop for the packaged-evidence audit opportunity, and run
  operator-attended macOS TUI, Memory, and Search acceptance against those exact
  bytes. Source validation is a pre-filter; it does not replace packaged
  acceptance of install, service, TTY, runtime, relocation, license, or feature
  paths.
- Classify operator findings as environment, documentation, or executable.
  Repeat only an affected row after environment setup; apply docs-only fixes
  through diff hygiene plus the docs gate; batch executable findings into one
  bounded remediation tranche with focused tests per commit and repeat only
  affected operator rows against a complete replacement generation. Do not run
  an aggregate per finding or remediation commit.
- After operator acceptance at the final SHA, run `release.v13` once and one
  uninterrupted authoritative `mix allbert.test release` from an isolated
  exact-SHA checkout. This is the sole aggregate/full-project execution across
  M0.a1-M9.b. Do not separately run inherited cumulative gates or
  `mix precommit`. A later executable/configuration/dependency/release-byte
  change invalidates that rejoin; a path-proved active-plan/request-flow edit
  needs only `git diff --check` plus the docs gate. Protected promotion verifies
  the bound draft
  release/asset IDs and digests, signs and publishes the same bytes, and makes
  the product tag/release immutable. Then fill the tap, validate the exact
  installed artifacts, archive the triad, and close roadmap/backlog state.
  Before publication, a source/byte fix replaces the whole draft generation
  under the same intended version; after publication, it requires a new patch
  version.

Acceptance:

- Definition of Done is complete at one source SHA and published-artifact
  digest set.

Per-milestone gate rules:

- Run focused suites first and append structured test-metrics records. During
  M0.a1-M9.b.5, use only the named owner-lane paths, named security rows, targeted
  static checks, inventory/tag/manifest checks, and required artifact/external
  smokes; do not substitute `mix allbert.test fast-local`, a partitioned
  high-coverage command, or another aggregate/full-project gate.
- Run compile warnings, formatter, Credo, Hex audit, and Dialyzer when the
  milestone changes code/contracts they cover. Intermediate workstream rejoins
  use those targeted static checks and never run full `mix precommit` merely
  because a commit or v1.2.5 candidate is being prepared. M9.b omits
  `mix precommit` because the authoritative release run contains its applicable
  ordered checks; it remains a compatibility shortcut, not separate release
  evidence.
- Security evals remain single-VM serial. Native artifacts, PTYs, real local
  models, configured messaging identities, OS service behavior, and published
  packages remain explicit external evidence.
- Do not execute `release.v1`, `release.v12`, `release.v121`, `release.v13`, or
  the full release gate during M0.a1-M9.b.5. Preserve and compose their definitions;
  M9.b executes only the newest cumulative `release.v13` and the authoritative
  full release gate after exact-prefix equivalence is proved.
- **Prefix equivalence is computed, not asserted.** The third pass found this
  referenced as a precondition with no mechanism, which would leave the team
  either running every gate (wall time) or skipping on belief. The rule: each
  gate definition emits its ordered step list; equivalence holds when the newer
  gate's step list contains the older gate's list as an exact ordered prefix,
  compared structurally at one SHA. The v1.2.5 comparison is recorded alongside
  its focused rejoin without a gate run; M9.b records the complete comparison
  alongside the two final aggregate runs. A mismatch is a blocking finding, not
  a prompt to run every inherited gate.
- The v1.2.6 candidate is tagged only after its focused/static/security rejoin at
  one clean pushed SHA. The sole version-specific and authoritative aggregate
  executions run against the same clean pushed v1.3 M9.b SHA. A docs-only
  descendant normally receives the applicable focused or final rejoin before
  its tag; the only exception is the path-proved active-plan/request-flow
  operator-instruction inheritance defined by M9.b and request-flow K6.

## External Smokes And Focused Validation

### v1.2.5

- Each native artifact: viewer summary/JSON/show, manifest reconciliation,
  executable without Mix/source tree, and no network access.
- macOS: resolved toolchain evidence and final dynamic-library closure; retain/
  remove OpenSSL patch from measured closure, not assumption.
- Packaged TUI: daemon present, daemon absent, occupied session, listener bind
  degradation with Web healthy, resize, Ctrl-C/EOF, confirmation, background
  fan-out/steering, Pi disconnect cancellation, and independent follow-up.
- Install through the published/tap path and re-run version/status/licenses/TUI
  smoke against the exact accepted digest.

### v1.3.0

- Real configured local model: extraction on the frozen corpus with zero hosted
  egress and deterministic fallback/abstention when unavailable.
- One warm local TUI plus Web/CLI: proposal review, temporal history, archive,
  Forget warning/confirmation, zero-shot and abstention.
- Search corpus: ingestion freshness, rebuild while serving prior generation,
  deletion/revocation, generation/revision cursor restart, scale/latency, and
  purge across every Search-owned generation/database/sidecar state.
- Configured real mapped DM channel where available: same-channel default,
  source labels, explicit one-query/cursor-chain cross-surface confirmation,
  pagination without reprompt, 300-second expiry/digest mismatch, canonical
  principal remap across both consumers, consumer origin/E2EE
  revocation isolation, and no raw query/snippet in audit or confirmation
  storage.
  Unconfigured channels are recorded as unavailable external evidence, never
  replaced by a fake product-acceptance claim.
- E2EE-origin row where a configured E2EE source exists: off by default,
  explicit plaintext-derived-index disclosure, opt-in, result, opt-out
  suppression, and repair.

FV is source-first for v1.3. After the M9.b.5 focused clean-SHA rejoin, first run
only the request-flow `FOV-*` manager/worker/composer qualification with a
disposable Home and real configured provider. It is a narrow behavior gate, not
Memory/Search or full-product acceptance. Once it passes, close that session and
start the complete source behavioral pre-filter at SV-0 with a new disposable
Home, one warm `mix allbert tui` session, and second-terminal
`mix allbert ...` commands. A source environment row repeats after setup repair;
a docs-only runbook correction receives diff hygiene plus the docs gate;
executable findings form one bounded focused-test tranche and repeat only
affected/dependent rows within the current phase. Do not inherit `FOV-*` as an
SV PASS, construct or rebuild native packages, or run an aggregate while either
source phase is failing.

After source acceptance, construct one complete exact-SHA provisional generation
and run packaged FV as release acceptance. It covers the same feature paths plus
install, service lifecycle, vault, TTY, ABI, relocation, and license behavior;
source success never substitutes for those bytes. Apply the same environment/
documentation/executable classification. A packaged executable or release-byte
fix replaces the complete generation, refreshes packaged latency rows, and
repeats only affected packaged FV rows. After operator acceptance, run one
cumulative plus authoritative aggregate rejoin at the final clean SHA, then
promote the already-qualified bytes. Aggregates never run per remediation commit.
Product validation uses normal surfaces/actions and disposable Homes; no
validation-only feature lands. v1.2.5 deliberately executes no aggregate gate.

## Security And Privacy Posture

- Packaging/license inspection adds no runtime authority and performs no
  network access.
- Attach remains local-only, Home/uid/version/protocol/token bound, size-bounded,
  and fail-closed for attached clients. A TUI session does not bypass Runtime,
  Security Central, actions, confirmations, or trace redaction.
- `SourceEnvelope`, Search rank, cursor, source ID, job ID, proposal ID,
  objective ID, and model output are never authority.
- Memory proposals are inert; only registered audited review actions make kept
  memory. Protected facts are individually reviewed.
- Search is local and read-only at the query boundary. `:search_manage` covers
  projection effects, not canonical deletion or cross-surface disclosure.
- Corpus authorization is checked again at proposal review and result return,
  not only at ingestion.
- Secrets are rejected/redacted before projection/proposal writes. Raw search
  text/snippets/filter operands, source excerpts, HMAC material, and forgotten
  content are excluded from traces/audits/logs/tests and durable confirmation/
  resume parameters; Search's safe summary is computed before requested-signal
  emission and fails closed to omission.
- E2EE indexing is an explicit privacy trade: decrypted local derived text
  exists in the Search projection. File permissions, backup exclusion, policy,
  reauthorization, and purge constrain exposure; encryption is not implied.
- Archive, canonical source deletion, projection purge, and Forget are separate
  typed/confirmed operations with separate consequences.

## Assumptions, Risks, And Stop Conditions

- **Best effort is intentionally not completeness.** A requirement for a
  complete universal SBOM/byte inventory is a new architecture/scope decision.
- **macOS needs one-time requalification.** Replacing floating Homebrew
  toolchains can alter dynamic closure. The OpenSSL patch is evidence-driven.
- **Manual append-only memory is a tighter contract.** Editing/removing old
  revisions is treated as corruption because silent mutation would destroy
  knowledge-time truth.
- **Forget cannot reach every copy.** Backups, exports, filesystem snapshots,
  and physical media are outside the active-store guarantee and must be
  disclosed before confirmation.
- **Search is plaintext-derived.** No encryption overhead is planned; local
  compromise/storage copying remains a residual risk documented to operators.
- **Search freshness is bounded, not synchronous.** Canonical reauthorization
  prevents stale disclosure, while physical repair may lag to the stated
  freshness/reconcile window.
- **FTS ordering is projection-revision-relative.** Scores may move after any
  ingestion. Cursors fail stale across generation or revision changes rather
  than promising continuity.
- **SQLite is one-writer per database.** Separate Search/Memory projections
  reduce contention and failure coupling but do not create a cross-database
  atomic transaction. v1.3 takes the Home from one database to three, inside
  the daemon that ADR 0091 makes the sole owner of writer discipline. v1.1's
  systemic root cause was resource ownership rather than fan-out logic, so
  LD 61 fixes each database's writer owner, supervision parent, and
  non-attached-CLI behavior before code lands. A non-attached CLI operation
  that depends on projection state attaches to the daemon or fails closed with
  guidance; surfaces never open projection databases directly. Two more
  writers is the risk this release most resembles v1.1.
- **Retiring legacy subsystems is not additive, so it is done by absorption.**
  LD 58 keeps every public action, setting, and request shape responding while
  routing behavior into the new model. A future release may delete the
  deprecated shapes under the ordinary Tier-2 process; v1.3 does not, because
  removal inside a frozen line is a separate operator decision.
- **`memory.auto_promote_sensitive_entries` shipped a promise the new model
  contradicts.** It is behaviorally inert and deprecated rather than deleted;
  the operator's stored value is not rewritten. Anyone
  reading the settings schema during v1.3 will still see the key and must find
  its deprecation note beside it.
- **External memory literature drifts.** Tests freeze Allbert behavior; no
  external service/API becomes a runtime dependency.
- **Purpose remains the bar.** If an outcome cannot be completed within the
  bounded design, implementation stops for operator disposition rather than
  adding substantial framework code or quietly cutting scope.

## Documentation Deliverables

Readiness edits live in the active triad and constraining ADRs. Implementation
updates the owning docs when behavior lands:

- ADR 0002, ADR 0076, ADR 0089; new ADR 0091, ADR 0092, and ADR 0093; ADR
  index.
- `docs/plans/v1.3-request-flow.md` maintained with actual message/state/error
  flows.
- `docs/developer/test-strategy.md`: `release.v121`/`release.v13` composition,
  lane ownership, exact-SHA equivalence, and metrics.
- `docs/developer/surface-contract.md`: TUI session and Search action/DTO/
  scoping contracts.
- `docs/developer/active-memory.md` and `docs/operator/active-memory.md`:
  claims/revisions, proposals, temporal history, archive/Forget, grants, and
  retrieval.
- `docs/operator/tui-channel.md`, `docs/operator/security-hardening.md`, and
  `docs/operator/export-import.md`: daemon attachment, plaintext-derived Search,
  backup exclusions, export/import/tombstone limitations, and purge.
- CLI mapping, entry-point UX, install, README, and one operator conversation-
  search guide. No developer Search sidecar, additional research document,
  generic subsystem manual, or extra milestone plan.
- License metadata, canonical inventory/catalog, root `NOTICE`, formula/bridge
  declarations, and artifact manifests at v1.2.5 implementation.
- `CHANGELOG.md` only when behavior actually lands.
- Roadmap/future-features lifecycle and triad archive only after each relevant
  published artifact is accepted.

## Definition Of Done

### v1.2.5

- License finalization/viewer is deterministic, offline, best-effort and
  managed-seam fail-closed, with the narrow Castore/Mozilla exception.
- All three exact artifacts carry/read target-truth manifests and required
  texts; toolchains and external/bundled components are recorded honestly.
- Daemon-backed TUI owns no runtime logic in the client, preserves legacy unary
  Attach behavior, fails safely without a daemon, and passes packaged TTY/
  confirmation/fan-out/Pi lifecycle rows.
- Dependency refresh/dispositions complete; docs, targeted static, focused,
  named security, and artifact/external rejoin evidence is clean.
- The `release.v121` definition and exact-prefix composition are recorded at one
  clean pushed SHA without aggregate execution; the exact binary is signed,
  published, tap-installed, and revalidated. Its contracts are executed
  cumulatively through `release.v13` at final M9.b closeout.

### v1.3.0

- Corpus identity, snapshot/page, digest, reauthorization, consumer/origin
  grants,
  remap/revocation/E2EE behavior, and Jobs.Managed are gate-proved.
- Append-only bi-temporal claim streams, legacy compatibility, corruption
  quarantine, complete projection rebuild, archive, and tombstone-first bounded
  Forget are gate-proved. Forget guarantees logical denial, canonical and
  active proposal-copy removal, and replacement/retirement of disposable
  projection generations; Repo secure-delete/checkpoint results and excluded
  unreachable pages/busy WAL/filesystem/backups/forensics are reported
  honestly, and its disclosure states that the originating conversation is
  retained and remains searchable, naming canonical delete as the further step. Same-Home key restore, missing-key quarantine, and destination
  exact-revision re-confirmation are proved.
- Consolidation is bounded, local, proposal-only, operator-authored-only,
  span-provenanced, sensitive-filtered, backpressured, and meets frozen
  precision/recall/abstention/update floors; one current Memory origin grant
  avoids per-run prompts without giving `:memory_propose` claim-mutation
  authority.
- Every LD 58 legacy subsystem is absorbed with its caller/action/settings DTO
  matrix proved: one compiled memory-index authority, one
  `(Home, memory-index-rebuild)` entry, no new discovery memory suggestions,
  exact-digest legacy-draft promotion through the claim writer, reversible
  prune/archive semantics, and a deprecated inert auto-promote key whose stored
  value is not rewritten.
- The one canonical conversation delete action (ADR 0093) supports
  `target_kind: message | thread`, exact preview/cascade, live-dependency
  blocking, best-effort known-core survivor disclosure, destructive
  confirmation, idempotent content-free outcomes, and immediate Corpus
  ineligibility ahead of projection repair; ADR 0093 is Accepted at that
  proving milestone.
- Measured latency bounds frozen at M1 are met and recorded for both consumers
  (Purpose outcome 9): Memory prompt-time lookup on the large-corpus fixture,
  and Search query latency at the frozen scale. An outcome claiming "bounded
  latency" closes on numbers from the metrics store, not on the absence of a
  complaint.
- Both feature switches ship with their stated defaults —
  `memory.consolidation.enabled` false, `search.enabled` true — and the
  asymmetry is documented wherever either appears.
- Authoritative Home export/backup contains neither the Search nor the Memory
  projection or their sidecars; restore rebuilds Search from Corpus and Memory
  from the Markdown claim streams.
- Each of the three databases has one named writer owner, and no non-attached
  path opens a second writer.
- Review is explicit, protected-item individual, frozen-batch partial,
  transition-ID/idempotently resumable across every claim/Repo crash point, and
  stale-source safe.
- Memory retrieval is deterministic, projection-backed, temporal, kept-only,
  Search-independent, prompt-budgeted, canonically revalidated at bounded
  top-N, and passes zero-shot plus negative/crash rows.
- Search Central is the only surface-facing **conversation** search engine
  (`search_memory` remains the claim-scoped memory entry point per LD 58); its
  safe lexical
  query, separate verified generations, checkpointed self-contained promotion,
  generation/revision-bound cursors, pre-write redaction, result
  reauthorization with bounded page refill/last-scanned cursors,
  policy-epoch/purge recovery, derived-export exclusion/restore rebuild, DM
  same-channel scope and one-query/cursor-chain elevation without durable raw
  query,
  freshness/reconcile, delete, maintenance, rebuild, and all-generation purge
  contracts pass.
- Managed consolidation/Search/memory-rebuild jobs are visible, unique per
  `(Home, managed_identity)`, operator-controllable, action-backed, and own
  dirty kicks/run history without another scheduler or wakeup process.
- Cross-consumer revocation/deletion/Forget matrix passes without leaking or
  silently deleting independently kept data.
- Dependency refresh/dispositions, docs, security evals, inventory/no-loss
  manifests, metrics, external smokes, and FV are complete.
- At the sole aggregate rejoin, `release.v13` and one authoritative release gate
  pass at one clean pushed M9.b SHA with exact-prefix evidence; `v1.3.0` is built
  natively, cosign-signed, published, tap-installed, and revalidated from the
  exact accepted artifacts.
